# AI Infrastructure Full-Stack Research & Architecture Workbench

> **Language / 语言**: [中文版 (Chinese)](README.md) | **English**

[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-blue.svg)](LICENSE)

An evidence-driven, source-verified full-stack AI Infrastructure research repository for Cloud and AI Solution Architects transitioning from cloud-native systems to **GPU / HBM / NVLink / RDMA / NCCL / 6D Parallel Training / Disaggregated Inference / Liquid-Cooled AI Factories**.

Every quantitative claim in this repository is backed by:
1. **First-hand upstream source verification** (`specification` / `implementation` with exact repository commit SHAs and file paths from `NVIDIA/nccl`, `deepseek-ai/DeepGEMM`, `deepseek-ai/DeepEP`, `vllm-project/vllm`, `sgl-project/sglang`, `pytorch/pytorch`, `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM`, `kvcache-ai/Mooncake`, `ai-dynamo/dynamo`, `deepseek-ai/3FS`, `Dao-AILab/flash-attention`, `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer`, `ROCm/rccl`, `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine`, `NVIDIA/nvidia-resiliency-ext`, `SchedMD/slurm`, `kubernetes-sigs/kueue`, etc.).
2. **Deterministic Python 3.12 standard-library experiments** (`own-offline`, zero third-party dependencies, reproducible via `--check` in `< 1s`).
3. **Bilingual Chinese & English documentation** (`README.md` <-> `README.en.md`, `docs/components/*.md` <-> `*.en.md`, and 7-Card 4:5 visual benchmark decks).

---

| Topic ID | Bilingual Deep-Dive Docs | Deterministic Experiment (`--check`) | Key Architectural Finding (`own-offline` + `implementation`) |
|---|---|---|
| `flashinfer-sm103-nvfp4-tmem-megamoe-ep16-kimi-ess-sm120-tilelang` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-sm103-nvfp4-tmem-megamoe-ep16-kimi-ess-sm120-tilelang.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-sm103-nvfp4-tmem-megamoe-ep16-kimi-ess-sm120-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_sm103_nvfp4_tmem_megamoe_ep16_kimi_ess_sm120_tilelang/`](experiments/flashinfer_sm103_nvfp4_tmem_megamoe_ep16_kimi_ess_sm120_tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`d08a1f732193`, `a4dc7d17fb6a`, `06da6cc640a0`, `506fac6203ae`, `ba12a8322385` & `4796d2effd9e`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`0bd33f953cf5` & `9253431f2a17`): expands `cake_nvfp4_attention` on `sm_103a` (GB300) from `432` to `480` TMEM columns (`P0_LO/HI = 448/456`, `P1_LO/HI = 464/472`) to overlap `MMA_PV(i-1)` with `Softmax(i)` (**`1.02x–1.05x` faster on GB300: `0.1145 -> 0.1094 ms` `1.05x` on `(4,8,4096)`**); optimizes `CakeMxfp8MegaMoeEp16` on 16xGB300 NVL72 via a single `fence.acq_rel.sys` + 16 `relaxed.sys` atomics per barrier and per-warp `cake_megamoe_dispatch_rows` copying each `6 KiB` BF16 row once (**`1.13x–1.18x` speedup: `377.5 -> 320.5 us` `1.18x` at `64 tok/rank` hotset50**); raises Kimi-K3 TP12 `K3_PERSIST_MIN_TOKENS` from `256` to `961` so `9 <= M <= 960` uses `k3_ess:grouped` (**`1.060x–1.080x` at `M=256, 512`**) and caches `_cudnn_version_supports_shape_override()` (`0.897 -> 0.072 us`, `-91.97%`); ships `minimax_h3_sm120_varlen_attention_fp8` (**`1.244x–1.404x` on RTX 5090**) and device-side FP64 `alpha` in `minimax_h3_nvfp4_out_proj` (**cutting GPU idle gaps `~60x` from `186.9–223.3 us` to `3.1–3.6 us`**); and fixes TileLang Ascend partial L1->L0 `l0_pitch=512 B` + ROCm `__tl_float_to_fp4` constants. |

| `flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1/`](experiments/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`2f158ed3c898`, `c3a7dd215803` & `0235de41d2e8`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`8f5129bd5a58`, `b7934818e67e` & `da680b857ba3`): optimizes Rubin (`sm_107a` VR200, `212` SMs = `2×106` SMs/die, `19.15 TB/s` DRAM) BF16-state GDN decode by cutting warp instructions **`~38%`** (identical copy-compute partition, 5-shuffle transpose-reduce, L2 `evict_last` lookahead `pf_dist = 212 * 25 // 3 = 1766` tiles) and allocating `[pool_size, HV, 2, V // 2, K]` via `CU_MEM_LOCATION_TYPE_DEVICE_LOCALITY_DOMAIN` (`ugpu_state_pool`), reaching **`88%` of `19.15 TB/s` (`516.5 us`, `1.41x`) at `B=2048` and `1.46x` (`12.3 us`) at `B=32`**; gates Rubin MLA NVFP4 decode PDL on `plan.max_splits > 1 and plan.items <= plan.units` (`54` units on 208-SM, `60` on 212-SM); ships KCP packed chunked state updates (`"wave_fill1"` `kcp_plan`, **`1.3058x–2.4679x` faster than FLA KCP** across 144 GB300 configs); and adds TileLang Ascend partial UB-to-L1 `Nd2NzGeometry`, `T.SimtVF` vector-local scalarization, and CUDA `cast_round="rs"` validation. |
| `vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm` | [中文](docs/components/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm.md) · [English](docs/components/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm.en.md) | [`experiments/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm/`](experiments/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm/run.py) | `vllm-project/vllm` (`82a0a5571be8`, `73ba8f510ab9`, `02f2b4e5a971` & `06ba27b71988`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`72fc295c48c7` & `845b6be3e03f`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`30a9763022a9` & `c07c92a460b0`): replaces `__float_as_uint(x)` bit-or in `vllm/csrc/topk_common.cuh` with opaque inline PTX `asm("mov.b32 %0, %1;" : "=r"(bits) : "f"(x));` so `nvcc` cannot lower it to FP `-|x|` (**restoring `qNaN` `0x7FC00000` key `0xFFC00000` instead of `-0.0` key `0x7FFFFFFF`**), retains `EngineCoreOutputs.aux_output` across idle ticks, releases CUDA blocks in MRV2 `empty_cache`, and bounds DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash SWA replay under PCP (`window >= 2 * pcp_size`, padding Rank 0 `127` rows and Rank 1 `2` rows to `num_padded = 129`); adds SGLang Inkling `tml-renderers` token-ID output parsing and Mistral3 subclassing; and moves 3 TRT-LLM request-scoped fields (`request_perf_metrics`, `disaggregated_params`, `time_breakdown_metrics`) to Python `CompletionOutput` (**shrinking `Result` pickle state from `14` to `11` fields**) + processor `chat_template` fallback. |
| `te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake` | [中文](docs/components/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake.md) · [English](docs/components/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake.en.md) | [`experiments/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake/`](experiments/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`1d2224e5b214` & `0b789a56804b`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`d7f4661a9e74`, `6d4c3e268001` & `c8529e650478`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`1d7dc7c5db18`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`4759b5c627e9`, `d6f76a944a4d` & `033ca26eb4ea`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`de86dfed63eb`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`24f36222fb72`): fuses RMSNorm + MXFP8 dual rowwise/columnwise scaling (`128x4` GEMM-swizzled scale indexing) and drains master-thread TMA S2G buffers via `cp_async_bulk_wait_group_read<BUFFS_NUM - 1>()` + `__syncthreads()` before slot reuse; introduces TorchTitan `ActorSystem` + `RDMAMicrobatch` and passes explicit `shape=p.shape, stride=p.stride()` to offloaded EMA `DTensor.from_local`; adds LMCache `bench trace-analyze` OTLP replay simulator (`4` deduped requests, `38` tokens, `12` hit tokens at `4:16`); enables Mooncake DFS-only replica placement and frees preempted extents immediately on unbind; and propagates NIXL UCX trace context while cleaning up Dynamo restore-candidate metadata. |
| `flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4/`](experiments/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`6103f5ea7c89`, `d51e1660b22c` & `9143f50102b0`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`baceed6676df`, `eaa9468d60f2` & `876a59e6eb80`): dispatches the fold-overlapped `"tailpipe"` kernel (`cake_mega_mhc_c549b064530c3765f9ba`) on `sm_107a` when `mhc_infer_tail_splits <= 22` (`T >= 385` on 208-SM R200, **`1.009x–1.024x` speedup on R200: `55.512 -> 54.208 us` at `T=4096` and `112.656 -> 111.103 us` at `T=8192`**); removes 6 unused headers from `norm.cuh` and proves across `242` GH200 PDL RMSNorm tests that removing `cudaGridDependencySynchronize()` from 2D norm kernels **fails `56/66` race cases (`100%` `33,792/33,792` corrupted elements on `264x128`)**; folds `43` XQA + `7` GQA Decode `sm_110a` (Thor) units into `9` headers (**`-14,131` net lines, `50/50` bit-identical SASS**); and generalizes TileLang Ascend packed FP4 MN-major alignment to `256 // operand_bits = 64`, inserts `1` same-iteration WAW barrier on overlapping shared writes (`0` on disjoint), and requires explicit K padding for unaligned CUDA MMA chunks. |
| `sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge/`](experiments/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`27ac614f051f`, `f05ad5ec0139` & `baf80c61b7ea`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`39a44fab74f4`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`e2b672312328`, `1b99dc5e8fce` & `5c465dda9da3`): introduces checkpoint-driven `response_template` serving adapters (`4` derived template variants, preserving `tool_close_token_ids` on thinking stops and switching post-reasoning JSON-schema tokens to `_verbatim=True`), replaces `logits_buffer.copy_(logits)` with Triton `cast_to_float32_logits` (**`2.8x` faster BF16->FP32 cast on GB300, `55.69 MiB` traffic at `[64, 152064]`**), and binds out-of-tree platforms via `GroupCoordinator` device + `"PrivateUse1"` custom ops; adds `Cosmos3EdgeForConditionalGeneration` to TensorRT-LLM (interleaved mRoPE `[24,20,20]` + `Cosmos3EdgeHfWeightMapper` filtering `17` diffusion-generator regexes); and emits vLLM streaming `kv_transfer_params` only on terminal `finish_reason` chunks while normalizing Rust `""` tool arguments to `"{}"` and reporting all invalid `logit_bias` keys. |
| `te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif` | [中文](docs/components/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif.md) · [English](docs/components/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif.en.md) | [`experiments/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif/`](experiments/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`15dcc848ecff` & `dd02f3f77c49`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`318d932e5bcf`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`01656768741d`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`323cf2b49646` & `3c7c1479151a`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`888887d35e37`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`f515971c9aaa`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`a0de541ea8c8`): adds SM100+ `128x128` wide-tile grouped MXFP8 requantization (**`4x` fewer tiles than `32x128`, coalesced `512 B` swizzled scale writes, and persistent `live_end` early exit running `512/4096 = 12.5%` tiles on a `2048`-row live prefix**); centralizes LMCache `transfer_with_address_kernel` (`MAX_CHUNKS_PER_LAUNCH = 4`, launch-0 `skip_prefix_n_blocks`, and pinned staging + `synchronize_device` for unpinned chunks); uses `nixlSerDes::exportMoveStr() &&` and `absl::InlinedVector` in NIXL UCX notifications (**cutting avg prep latency `-32.14%` `2.8 -> 1.9 us` and P99 post `-25.0%` `12.0 -> 9.0 us`**); and fixes TorchTitan Flex `FX_ANNOTATION` per-layer recompiles (`14 -> 1` graph, exact `6.9951515` loss), 4-tuple PP unpack, RL `GenerationEngine` split, Mixtral Transformers 5.0.0 compatibility, Mooncake DFS KV bench, and Dynamo `DYN_DISABLE_OPENAI_STRICT_SCHEMA_VALIDATION`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi/`](experiments/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`58232f576ad7`, `aec44bda63e6` & `c0e51ae29b19`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`cb26ee0fafe5` & `2573d470798a`): dispatches two-heads-per-CTA `T=1` decode (`l8r4x2`, `(B*HV+1)//2` CTAs when `B*HV >= 32*sm_count`, **`1.18x` on B200 `243.4 -> 206.1 us` and `1.13x` on GB300 `228.0 -> 201.7 us` at `HV=80, B=256, T=1`**) and replaces dual 5-step warp butterfly reductions in `splitv8_tile8` with paired-row half-warp reduce-scatter + all-gather (**cutting `__shfl_xor_sync` calls per substep `10 -> 6`, `-40.0%`**); chains `L=8` distinct `ValueProfileArena` slots per L2 flush under `cold-chain:8` (**amortizing B300's `6.38 us` single-launch floor to pick `SplitK(64,8,4,12)` at `3.74 us` over `WarpSplitK` at `5.17 us` and dropping `pdl=1` `>5%` misses `21/30 -> 1/30`**); compiles one `@cute.jit` TVM-FFI `launch` entry per Frost tactic (**`4.22x` public API `260.85 -> 61.85 us` and `8.75x` runner `217.82 -> 24.90 us` on B300 `T=1` `MXFP4 TC128`**); and rejects `step != 1` in TileLang `VectorizeLoop` while preserving signed `int64` `T1` in `cuda::atomic_ref`. |
| `sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942/`](experiments/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`222676c8d7bd` & `4ccdca1e8492`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`ea226cf78267`, `5f7542d7627f` & `15dc838f9bf5`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`18df4cd9a98b` & `29cf023770a0`): fuses CuTe-DSL KDA prefill short conv + indexed state pool update (`-7,214` legacy lines) with a 1-warp-vote guard for `min(h) < -2.0` on fast `silu(x) = h + h * tanh.approx(h)` (**bounding error to `< 1.35%` for $h \ge -2$ while preventing `>3953%` cancellation at $h=-6$**) and widens GVR v2 Top-K `QC` `96 -> 160` while saving **`4,096 B` SMEM/CTA** at `NBH=1024`; separates static `graph_num_tokens`, global `cache_tokens`, and scalar `moe_local_token_count` in SGLang GLM-5.3 Flash padded CP BCG, caches DSV4.1 MegaGate `(valid, image)` row masks once per forward (**`120 -> 2` mask ops across 60 MoE layers, `60.0x` reduction**), and maps `SGLANG_TIMEOUT_KEEP_ALIVE` (`s -> ms`) in Granian HTTP/1.1; and keeps ROCm `gfx942` PagedAttention head offset in 32-bit `int32` (**eliminating SGPR spill `NaN`s**) while unifying `gfx950` FlyDSL `GQA-{6,8,16}` (`-875` lines + `base_byte_offset`). |
| `megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring/`](experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`18bc49d2f6bc`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`c9c92255465b` & `2199b7166417`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`43d1bb98014e` & `d681f48b43ad`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`ff9185d419e1` & `a81b4e5aea0c`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dd1217b6c461`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`a9f1f7a76f46`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`906e89d3a304` & `45cc9e8a1a9e`): gathers only rowwise MXFP8 planes in `Phase.FORWARD` and columnwise planes in non-recompute `Phase.BACKWARD` (**cutting per-phase All-Gather payload by `50.0%`, `34.60 -> 17.30 MB` on `4096x4096`**) and clamps over-capacity TE Triton `row_id >= num_out_tokens` to `-1`; reconfigures TorchTitan Muse Spark 816B-A42B GB300 RL trainer from `256` to **`128` GPUs (`FSDP=8 x TP=4 x CP=4 x EP=16`, `8.0x` smaller expert FSDP group, `28%` MFU at `131K`)** and passes packed `2x2` sequence length (`256` vs `1024` at 256px) to Flux `get_schedule`; frees excess LMCache lookup tail locks in `[min_hits, server_hits)`; reads `clock_gettime` once per Mooncake RDMA post chain (**cutting 256-WR lock hold `6.3 -> 1.9 us`, `-69.84%`**); and aligns NIXL ring headers to `256 B` (`512 B` total) while batching Dynamo output-token atomics every `64` tokens (**`64.0x` reduction**). |
| `flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc/`](experiments/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`b27f6cae7da4`, `c56cd4fb7222`, `c01aa7779bbb` & `6b38488084e3`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`35ee29507557` & `fe8c34618ffb`): checks scalar invariants and compares private detached views (`self.inputs`) with immutable `dataclasses.replace(metadata, ...)` in `_CudnnPrefillPlan.prepare()` (**cutting H100 NVL host `plan()` latency by `13.90%` `79.12 -> 68.12 us` at `B1,Q128,KV8192` and `14.93%` `77.38 -> 65.83 us` at `B4,Q512,KV512` (`plan+run` `-9.80%`), while catching in-place `as_strided_((2,4),(1,2))` mutations on retained metadata**); inserts `fence.proxy.async.shared::cta;` across 6 split-K slab collectors (`_sks_sb2`) and refreshes `st.global.L1::no_allocate.L2::evict_first.v8.b32` lowerings across `247` `sm_100a`/`103a`/`107a` build entries (**`1.008x–1.016x` GB300 & `1.003x–1.037x` GB200**); folds 3 FP32 wgrad MoE clones via `-DWGRAD_OUT=32` (`8 -> 5` programs, **`-4,954` combined net lines**, bitwise-identical SASS across `26` libraries); and fixes TileLang `NVRTCAdapter.from_database` `libpath` + `VectorizeLoop` `analyzer->Clone()`. |
| `sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope/`](experiments/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`348347ce3e93`, `4942e2a02145` & `49bd803025eb`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`e6451c1a956d` & `194abb8e54a8`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`b1c66e55450d` & `d143a9de3915`): replaces `reciprocal_approximate_ftz(SFValue)` (`rcp.approx.ftz.f32`) with exact `exp2f_rcp(fp8SFVal)` in `cvt_warp_fp16_to_mxfp8` (**returning finite normal $2^{127}$ for subnormal `E8M0` scale $2^{-127}$ and eliminating `0.0 * inf = NaN` in near-zero blocks `vecMax in (0, 448 * 2^-127]`**), reads `mapping.world_size` under Ray (`TLLM_DISABLE_MPI`), and gates PWCG NVTX probes on `TLLM_PWCG_NVTX_PROBE=1`; loops `for (int i = 0; i < mmq_x; i += nwarps)` in SGLang GGUF `moe_q` (**loading all `64/64` rows of `tile_y_ds` instead of `8/64` at `nwarps=8`**), uses PTX `mul.rn.f32` to preserve subnormals under SM90 `-use_fast_math`, and synchronizes `self.stream` after `self.outputs.clear()` in Cosmos Dreams `CausalDecodeQueue.__exit__`; and shares a single `get_rope()` cache bounded to `max_model_len=8192` across 32 ROCm Muse Spark layers in vLLM (**`2,048.0 -> 4.0 MiB`, `512.0x` reduction**) + honors `VLLM_WORKER_SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` on immediate aborts. |
| `megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid/`](experiments/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`f9d9bdcc6e09` & `92641a421f40`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`9ea05cf2e8dc`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`a0ff4701284d`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dae22d472e31` & `b66bb2b5f650`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`f3673f6be3f4` & `70badd6aad36`): zeroes padded token rows from the `[1]` `int32` device tensor `gpu_view.real_token_count` during CUDA-graph replay in `zero_padding_rows_kernel.py` (**recovering `32.0x` FP8 scale precision `7.0 -> 224.0` when `72/128` rows are real**) and pools global-batch K3 `qb_histogram`; enables FP8 weight workspace caching in `torch.compile`d TE `Linear` (**cutting 8-microbatch step weight casts `8 -> 1`, `-87.50%`, peak memory `452 MiB` eager / `539 MiB` compiled**); aligns LMCache SWA full-prompt prefix replay (`12 == 12`) to chunk boundaries (`(12-1)//8*8 = 8`, unlocking `[8, 12)` and **eliminating 4-token OOB retrieves**); advances Mooncake `notify_send_wr_id_` only after `ibv_post_send()` succeeds (**`0` in-flight slot `0` overwrites at the 257th send**) + diagnoses container PID VMM failures; and appends a 16B `treq` tag to NIXL 26B `tctx` notifications (`42B` frame). |
| `flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline/`](experiments/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`21ac136220`, `c0e51ae29b` & `b90521314b`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`4b2032a6ce`, `1bafce1a37` & `d9a31ddd1c`): adds `sm_107a` Hidden-4096 fused mHC quantized output variants (`sf_layout="fp8_group128"` `[T, 4096]` + `[T, 32]` FP32 UE8M0 power-of-two scales and `sf_layout="nvfp4"` `[T, 2048]` + `[T, 256]` FP8-E4M3 scales at **`3.5556x` compression**, saving **`1.0 MiB` HBM re-reads at `T=128`** with bitwise-identical base outputs); compiles Frost low-latency MoE into a single TVM-FFI `Pipeline.launch` entry (`"mx-tc-ffi-v1"`, **`2.96x–4.22x` Public API & `5.69x–8.75x` Prepacked Runner speedup on B300 `T=1` SwiGLU: `MXFP4 TC128` `217.82 -> 24.90 us`**); ships StepFun Fused MoE Tile-128 MXFP8 FC1/FC2 (**`1.047x` GB300 & `1.071x` B200 at `T=1024`**); and fixes TileLang transitive pipeline copy worklist `[1, 0]`, thread-domain conflict expansion, and Ascend PTO MAD predicate dedup ($2^{32} \to 1$ node). |
| `sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words/`](experiments/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`6d6c9cd4ae`, `494eeecb6c` & `f9129915a3`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`1586d85375` & `8d1c7e0b26`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`34b2954482`): scans non-`Parameter` module attributes via `(device, untyped_storage().data_ptr())` in SGLang `OffloaderV2` to automatically pin view-aliased weights (e.g., KDA `qkv_conv1d.weight`) on GPU and bypasses `_VERDICTS` under `torch.compiler.is_compiling()` (**collapsing 40-layer Dynamo recompiles `40 -> 1`**); unscatters `[P, B, Sp, 3, H, D]` directly with `uint4` (8 BF16) loads/stores in TRT-LLM `ulyssesPackedQkvPostUnscatterKernel` (**cutting post-All-to-All HBM traffic by `50.0%`: `301.99 -> 150.99 MB`**) + `checkInt32SumProduct` overflow guards; and aligns vLLM Rust `tokenize_bad_words` (`EmptyBadWord` pre-tokenization rejection, `HashSet` dedup, `max_num_bad_words` cap, and `with_space[0] != without_space[0] && len == len` filter). |
| `te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma` | [中文](docs/components/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma.md) · [English](docs/components/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma.en.md) | [`experiments/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma/`](experiments/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`39a8b879df` & `76ae4b0981`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`0d441be069`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`99d469be5d` & `14e329032b`): fuses NaN-safe `_fold_block_argmax` (treating `NaN` as maximum with first-NaN/first-tie winner) into TE `parallel_cross_entropy(..., return_argmax=True)` (**saving `125.25 MiB/rank` logits HBM re-reads at `T=4096, V=128256, TP=8` against `16 KiB` all-gather, `8016.0x` ratio**) and zeroes `tensor_base_for_scales` on multi-tensor Grouped MXFP8 (`4096 -> 2048` offset fix); collapses 61-layer × 32-block `BLNHC` transfers via LMCache `uniform_layer_pitch` `cudaMemcpy3DBatchAsync` (**`1,952 -> 32` descriptors, `61.0x` reduction**); and splits objects $> 4\text{ GiB} - 64\text{ KiB}$ (`4,294,901,760 B` `cuObject` token limit) into S3 Multipart RDMA transfers in NIXL (**`9 GiB -> 3` parts, `64 GiB -> 17` parts**) + GPUNetIO peer-VRAM two-SGE WRITE pairing (`64 -> 32` WQEs). |
| `flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang/`](experiments/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`ddc9524d9f`, `34d28b3232` & `85e886c875`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`cb26ee0558` & `2573d472cb`): retains `KcpSaved.carry` under `boundary_mode="saved"` (`12.0–80.0 MiB/rank` at $H=64$ when $P>1$, `0 B` when $P=1$) to bypass backward K1 re-walk and carry merge (**`0.9950x–1.0387x` B3 speedup across 144 GB300 rows, `1.0285x` s1 / `1.0284x` s2 geomean on 8 sampled high-CP rows, `0` rows `>2%` slower**); loads K before V with `CacheHint::evict_first` and 1-instruction `prmt.b32` (`0x5140`) in Cake QSA NVFP4 decode (**`1.127x` peak at `rows=32` on H100 `36.42 -> 32.32 us` NHD & `38.83 -> 34.45 us` HND, `1.0852x` mid-batch geomean**); adds SM107 residual `evict_first` and `_claim_launch_epochs` grid-sharing guards in MegaMHC; and rejects `step != 1` in TileLang `VectorizeLoop` while fixing signed `int64` `AtomicMax(-5, 3) == 3` and `AtomicStore` casts. |
| `sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm/`](experiments/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`8b9b992e41` & `16aa816f1b`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`90d4dcbb8f` & `222676cad0`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`d561af5dfc` & `37cf712552`): maps negative/OOB expert IDs via unsigned `sentinel_expert_bucket` to bucket 0 and publishes `int2(num_tokens_past_padded, inactive_prefix_blocks)` in Marlin WNA16 MoE (**skipping `6/16` `37.5%` inactive prefix blocks via single `int2` load**) while resetting `heartbeat_failures.pop(bootstrap_addr, None)` on disaggregated prefill disconnect; fuses SM100 QuACK BF16 gate/up GEMM with SwiGLU epilogue in TensorRT-LLM (**cutting MiniMax-H3 activation HBM traffic `560.0 -> 112.0 MiB` `-80.0%`, saving `448.0 MiB/layer`**) and integrates FlashInfer KDA `bt16` prefill (**`-5,706` lines**); and guards vLLM Mamba prompt-tail re-caching (`block_hash_num_tokens == num_tokens`, **`3 -> 1` step `3.0x` sibling admission**) while aligning V2 speculative draft RoPE caches via `_CACHE_ONLY_ROPE_TYPES`. |
| `megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl/`](experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`18bc49d2f6` & `da2c0ec614`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`c9c9225546` & `2199b71664`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dd1217b6c4` & `c81f4ad486`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`a9f1f7a76f`) & `LMCache/LMCache` (`ff9185d419`): unshards only rowwise in `Phase.FORWARD` and columnwise in `Phase.BACKWARD` under Megatron MFSDPv2 MXFP8 (**cutting per-step All-Gather traffic by `50.0%`: `303.1875 -> 151.5938 MiB`**) and snapshots all 5 mutable fields in `RecomputeSegment` (`mtp_input_mask` preserved); clamps TE Triton mask-map permute `row_id >= num_out_tokens` to `-1` (**blocking `17` over-capacity row writes**) and propagates `record_stream` across all 7 `GroupedTensor` buffers; reads the clock once per `ibv_post_send` chain in Mooncake RDMA (**cutting 256-WR lock hold `6.3 -> 1.9 us` `3.3158x`**) + shard-free DFS eviction & `DeferDeletion`; aligns NIXL `sharedRingBuffer` to `256B` (**`-99.9%` cross-core acquire loads**); and cuts LMCache lookup-to-query latency **`5.40 -> 0.80 ms` (`6.75x`)**. |
| `flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang/`](experiments/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`2202dd75c1`, `2706a076ac` & `680ca14fa2`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`1c1ca63876`, `59c97a8966` & `34cd033ab2`): adds learnable per-head `attention_sink` to 64-head DSA (`m_init = sink[h] * log2(e)`, `dsink_row = -exp2(sink[h]*log2(e) - lse) * delta`) and widens 12 `bwd_pre` kernels from `32` to `128` threads (**`1.0504x` step speedup on GB300 `glm53_b` `32.899 -> 31.321 ms`, `1.0238x` on R200 `doc_4096` `36.701 -> 35.847 ms`**); refactors Cake Dense GEMM into 10 family subpackages + `_router.py` (**removing `31,148` net lines `-69.98%` while preserving `1.0008x` B200 and `1.0005x` GB300 geomean parity**); adds `[B, T, H, D]` fixed-batch `cake_chunk_kda` (`_FixedBatchCuSeqlensCache`) + `chunk_kda_bwd` workspace pass-through; and adds TileLang Blackwell `2x2` TMEM layout guards, CP `cuStreamWaitValue64` sync, and liveness borrow verification. |
| `sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope/`](experiments/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`2dfae94da7` & `db1e2e2073`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`3092cf2116` & `fb7461d525`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`97131637e9` & `bf77355849`): replaces `fused_moe_lora_shrink` `split_k` atomic partials with an in-kernel `BLOCK_K` loop and forces `use_fused_gate=False` on `GGUFUninitializedParameter`; scales TRT-LLM speculative `target_logits /= max(T, 1e-5)` before rejection sampling (**`0.0` TVD vs `0.2156`, restoring acceptance `54.82% -> 81.33%` `+26.51` pts at `T=0.5`**) and searches per-block NVFP4 `E4M3` weight scales over `[0.75, 1.00]` (**`-31.11%` W4A4 MSE**); and eliminates vLLM incremental RoPE `repeat_interleave(2, dim=-1)` via 4D broadcast (**saving `2.0 MiB` scratch/layer**) while passing post-route `num_valid_tokens` to FlashInfer MoE (**cutting `EP=8` scheduled rows `4,096 -> 640`, `-84.38%`**). |
| `torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](docs/components/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](docs/components/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl/`](experiments/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl/run.py) | `pytorch/torchtitan` (`2fddc6b7a0`, `21db340cba` & `eb48bab350`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`4e8a71b902`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`9049704a2c`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`c2f4580b18`): compiles DeepSeek-V3/V4.1 MLA `apply_rotary_emb` and `swiglu` with FP32 `RMSNorm` reduction (**cutting per-layer HBM traffic by `672.0 MiB` `-52.27%`/`-60.00%` and raising GB300 671B-A37B throughput `4,120 -> 4,785 tok/s/GPU` `+16.14%`**) and fixes `q_a_proj == None` state-dict mapping; rebinds TE `GroupedLinear` `SWIZZLED_128x4` scale views before grouped GEMMs (**`0.0` vs `1.42e-1` L2 grad error**); adds Mooncake `EvictUnbucketedKeys()` (**`100%` orphan reclaim, `320.0 MiB` freed on 10K keys**); and falls back to `cuDevicePrimaryCtxRetain` for `cuMemCreate` VMM buffers in NIXL Libfabric (**`0% -> 100%` registration**). |
| `flashinfer-tp-moe-finalize-muse-norm-kcp-carry-qsa-tilelang` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-tp-moe-finalize-muse-norm-kcp-carry-qsa-tilelang.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-tp-moe-finalize-muse-norm-kcp-carry-qsa-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-tp-moe-finalize-muse-norm-kcp-carry-qsa-tilelang/`](experiments/flashinfer-tp-moe-finalize-muse-norm-kcp-carry-qsa-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`0e832d0636`, `787132c835`, `ddc9524d9f`, `85e886c875` & `34d28b3232`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`c412c2d4b1`, `f5c6b44cfe` & `ed06ed1f01`): fuses Top-8 MoE Finalize + Lamport Ring AllReduce (`eo`/`onehop`/`rsag`/`token1`/`tokown`) + RMSNorm (`HIDDEN=4096, TOPK=8, ALIGNMENT=256`) on `sm_100a` (`TP2, TP8`) and `sm_103a` (`TP2, TP4`) while rejecting mismatched `sm_100a TP4` & `sm_103a TP8` (**`1.334x` on GB300 TP4 `T=256`: `50.55 -> 37.91 us`; `1.244x` on B200 TP8 `T=256`: `65.96 -> 53.04 us`**); adds Cake Muse Spark norm kernels (**`1.818x` on B200 V1 `M=16384`: `438.82 -> 241.37 us`; `1.702x` on GB300 V1 `M=16384`: `403.39 -> 237.02 us`; `1.375x` on GB300 `rmsnorm_sigmoid_gate`: `2.42 -> 1.76 us`**); enables KCP `SAVED_CARRY_DEFAULT=1` retaining `72.0 MiB` on `Q4_CP16` `P=2` (**`1.0387x` B3 speedup: `9.788 -> 9.423 ms`**); adds SM107 3D TMA `L2::cache_hint` + whole-storage `_claim_launch_epochs` and H100 QSA NVFP4 K-first + `1-prmt` (**`1.127x` at `rows=32`**); and adds TileLang `Int64Promoter` before `RegionsMayConflict`, `MakeStridedSlice` `elem_offset` preservation, and periodic buffer sibling guards. |

| `sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream/`](experiments/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`a3d00eff58`, `7e5256d1ba`, `ca72dad953` & `68623cae92`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`1c7fadcfbe` & `271ed9f22a`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`6ae1051bd9` & `5ea2c7ff1e`): computes `contains_last_prefill_chunk` on the host in `ForwardBatch.init_new` and propagates it across TBO child batches (`0` GPU-to-CPU syncs); balances Uvicorn tokenizer workers with per-worker `SO_REUSEPORT` listeners (`--enable-http-worker-reuseport`, `uvicorn>=0.51`); answers all health probes skipped by a busy scheduler on the Rust response ring via a single `frame_health_ack()`; gathers Mamba ping-pong indices via `track_position`; removes obsolete TRT-LLM speculative max-draft CUDA Graph capture (`original_max_draft_len`) and unpacks `(mm_data, mm_embeddings)` for Responses API `input_image` parts; and adds vLLM Rust `--exclude-tools-when-tool-choice-none` / `max_tool_calls: Option<usize>` plus a shared `ChatStreamProcessor` between streaming and `online_derenderer`. |
| `te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](docs/components/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](docs/components/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl/`](experiments/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`53e241c5a1` & `c3081a5f2d`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`8899cee5bc` & `5e27dc87b8`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`741b613059`, `786010f4d6` & `32f8aa90b7`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`7ff6bf7f72` & `292e8391b7`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`d4f72ffe6a` & `8882e2d348`): adds `reset_parameters()` deferred init across `te.ops` fusible ops and narrows the pre-cuDNN 9.26 THD learnable `dSink` guard so `head_dim_v in {64, 128, 256}` specialized kernels stay enabled; sets `NCCL_GRAPH_REGISTER=0` for normal Ulysses CP on `NCCL < 2.32.3` and preserves `AddedToken.special=False` delimiters (`<think>`, `<tool_call>`) via `add_tokens`; decouples LMCache `fs_native` read depth with a `32 MiB` default byte budget (`read_max_bytes_in_flight`), supports dim-0-padded KV views in isolated IPC, and adds Ascend Mamba KV group edits; eliminates bilateral Mooncake EFA active handshake deadlocks via `active_setup_mutex_` + `generation_` and adds configurable `2..60s` etcd lease TTLs; and sets `EVLOOP_NO_EXIT_ON_EMPTY` in NIXL Redis while bounding GPUNetIO notification parsing and preserving embedded `\x00` bytes. |
| `flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4/`](experiments/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`cd6c1885a8`, `a3f2525e39`, `3b65505f40` & `edfb3f7f88`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`c018848da6`): eliminates CUDA 13.2–13.4 register spills across **1,440 SM120 Cake MegaMoE JIT instances per toolkit (`0/1440` spills across 11 CUDA 13 releases, `<=168` regs, `0 B` stack on proxy/GDAKI)** via `_CCCL_ATOMIC_UNSAFE_AUTOMATIC_STORAGE`, per-GIN modules, `m128` 1-step K unroll, and a 15-bit signed ABI cap `ACTIVE_ROWS_FIELD_MAX = 0x7FFF` (`max_rows <= 32640`); adds opt-in 4-wave GQA decode KV splitting (`FLASHINFER_TRTLLM_GEN_MULTI_WAVE_KV_SPLIT=1`) when single-wave splits `<= 2`, achieving **`1.86x` geomean speedup on B200 64k lognormal decode and `2.02x` peak at `B=38` 288k lognormal (`1566 -> 775 us`)** with exact scratch fallback; ships 133 Kimi-K3 AttnRes round-2 programs (`161` libraries: **`1.0888x` on GB300 `serving_m5_k5`, `1.1034x` on B200 `k_sweep_m1_k6`**) and self-contained NVFP4 attention (`0.9975x` GB300 parity); and replaces `video_np.tobytes()` with `memoryview(video_np.reshape(-1))` in TRT-LLM VisualGen (**saving a `711.91 MiB` host allocation and copy**). |
| `sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe/`](experiments/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`f7d4f06242`, `bc412213c1`, `97945831f9`, `620718c8ea` & `2c7a9e3b50`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`b0e1f8052a`): precomputes DeepSeek-V4.1 `WindowLayout.history_src` once per batch (**`60x` fewer 2D fancy-index gathers: `60 -> 1`**), caches `low_ratio_k_slots` and `FastIndexPlan` per compression ratio (`r in {4, 8}`) on `DSV4Metadata` (**`24x` fewer index-k gathers across 48 layers: `48 -> 2`**), and restores sparse prefill when `request_window` is active; orders `UnifiedCacheLinker` (`MooncakeDirectLinker`, `UMBPDirectLinker`) async offloads behind both `current_stream` and an `_offload_ready_event` recorded on `forward_stream` (**eliminating `12 us < 25 us` read-before-write KV corruption**); and adds vLLM monolithic MoE `supports_monolithic_kernels` + `prepare_monolithic_bias()` alongside SGLang DSV4.1 `vision_topk` unpacked-ID guards under routing simulation. |
| `megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl/`](experiments/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`29e1a7b669`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`9698a73362` & `a242475253`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`a6accee6d8`, `21aa6ac06c` & `45486917f1`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`7a05855674`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`03d9b74648`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`5004f3fa80`): models Gated DeltaNet (GDN) prefill CUDA graph static `cu_seqlens` buffers as a single-update GDP (`num_householder=1`, `64` chunk descriptors at `T=4096`); moves gradient clipping into TorchTitan's `OptimizersContainer` as an optimizer-owned `GradientClipper` shared with `TorchFT` and delegates `_LinearFunction.spmd_typecheck` to `einsum` bilinearity (`Partial @ Replicate -> Partial`) for LoRA under TP; replaces LMCache's fixed `max_in_flight=8` with `--l2-prefetch-inflight-watermark=0.5` (**`4.0x` higher `8 MiB` concurrency: `32 vs 8`**), fixes `VmmCudaIPCWrapper` dim-0-padded KV pool byte span (`3584 B` vs `2048 B`), and binds the primary CUDA context in `stream_ops`; enables Mooncake CPU-to-GPU TCP memory selection; adds NIXL 64-bit `xfer_request_id` & `xfer_execution` telemetry; and propagates Dynamo multimodal image token errors as `None` instead of `Some(0)`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ/`](experiments/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`067b36956d`, `1ff146212d`, `4e4c41da67`, `15c83f1c36`, `75b30ffdbf` & `e2456a4eea`): ships 37 per-architecture (`sm_100a`/`sm_103a`/`sm_107a`) NVFP4-transport all-reduce + RMSNorm programs (`_rso`, `_rst`, `_qp`, `_sop`, `_pf`) reaching **`1.0718x` geomean on B200 (`1.0942x` peak at `8K x 6144` `NVFP4 -> NVFP4`: `77.36 -> 70.70 us`)**; casts to `float` before squaring in MNNVL one-shot fused allreduce + RMSNorm so FP16 activations with `|x| > 255.94` do not overflow to `inf` (**restoring Qwen3.5-27B FP16 TP=2 RL score from `0.000` to `0.698`**); merges SM100/SM103 Sage-FP8 block-sparse attention into 1 launch (**`1.30x–1.40x` faster on GB300, `1.23x` faster fused quantizers**); adds `trtllm_fused_moe_workspace_size` and caller-owned `workspace_buffer=` (**cutting B300 autotuning peak memory from `3,579.9 MiB` to `896.8 MiB`, `-2,683.1 MiB`**); and folds Kimi-K3 SiTU MoE by **`-17,018` lines (`-22.60%`)** with 100% identical SASS. |
| `sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora/`](experiments/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`028de2c5d4` & `62b4127668`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`a0141d0e56` & `00c184e1f8`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`e8e43c6a09`, `a0a645abda` & `c84edac4cb`): lowers DeepSeek-V4.1 `mxfp8_prefill_autotune_min_tokens` from `4096` to `1` on FlashInfer CuTeDSL (**unlocking `14/14` tuned decode/prefill buckets vs `2/14`**) and deduplicates ComfyUI Flux ControlNet residuals via `_memo[id(value)]` (**`5.7x` lower IPC pickle & `shard_latents_for_sp` work across `57` blocks**) with `_SESSION_SKIP_EXTRA` step isolation; retires **`35` legacy executor `MpiTag` enum entries** and 6 compatibility files while enabling `ENABLE_BOLT_PREMERGE_CONSUME = true` and nightly release BOLT variant publishing; and adds `TokenClassificationHeadWithLoRA` with `AllPool(output_sizes_fn)` slicing (`[[12,3],[8,9],[15,5]]`), shared-GPU NIXL prefill-first barrier, and ROCm EAGLE3 `VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1`. |
| `te-grouped-mlp-align-megatron-sched-titan-rope32-nixl-proxy-mooncake` | [中文](docs/components/te-grouped-mlp-align-megatron-sched-titan-rope32-nixl-proxy-mooncake.md) · [English](docs/components/te-grouped-mlp-align-megatron-sched-titan-rope32-nixl-proxy-mooncake.en.md) | [`experiments/te-grouped-mlp-align-megatron-sched-titan-rope32-nixl-proxy-mooncake/`](experiments/te-grouped-mlp-align-megatron-sched-titan-rope32-nixl-proxy-mooncake/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`9d9bd9e5c4` & `33f3b35619`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`e4eb89b3bb`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`48bbce4434` & `efdde5edaf`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`18651e8f19`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`8e26089d3f`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`fda3a883c0`) & `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`a4fe83974b`): replaces `NVTE_CUTEDSL_FUSED_GROUPED_MLP` with automatic `GroupedLinear(split_alignment=256/128)` `GroupedMLP_CuTeGEMMGLU` fusion and fixes `onnx_cs_quantize_fp8_op` fake-tensor scale shape to 0-D `()`; migrates Megatron LR/WSD step derivation to `SchedulerConfig`; sets `scaling_factor=32.0` on Llama-3.2 1B/3B (**fixing `17/32` & `35/64` rotary frequencies, `0.781 rad` error at pos 8192, and cutting max logit diff to `7e-7`**) and isolates per-module `_MissingKernel[<module>]` subclasses; consolidates `lmcache.inprocess.v1.transfer_channel`; forwards visible stop token IDs (`[2,7,200002,200012]`) in Dynamo sidecars; runs NIXL EP over an 8-channel / 8-thread CPU proxy (**`59.0 GB/s` / `103%` on H100, `53.3 GB/s` / `109%` on GB200**); and exposes Mooncake `ConductorClient` via `conductor_c.h` C ABI and a `Send + Sync` Rust crate. |

| `sglang-mi35x-ibv-tool-coercion-trtllm-fp8-custom-mask-vllm-rust-cli` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-mi35x-ibv-tool-coercion-trtllm-fp8-custom-mask-vllm-rust-cli.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-mi35x-ibv-tool-coercion-trtllm-fp8-custom-mask-vllm-rust-cli.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-mi35x-ibv-tool-coercion-trtllm-fp8-custom-mask-vllm-rust-cli/`](experiments/sglang-mi35x-ibv-tool-coercion-trtllm-fp8-custom-mask-vllm-rust-cli/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`7e0e259435`, `a2e4ffbcea` & `6b6c61314d`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`5f90e8939d` & `1c7fadcfbe`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`2916b4b4a6`, `bc4794ef9a` & `8a21d0a328`): auto-sets `MOONCAKE_DISABLE_HIP_DMABUF=1` on AMD MI35x (`gfx950`) to bypass `rocm_dmabuf` via `ibv_reg_mr`, coerces whole-number float tool arguments (`"3.0" -> 3`, `"1e2" -> 100`, rejecting `"inf"/"NaN"`), and deduplicates Flux ControlNet `controlnet_single_block_samples` by `id(residual)` (`0` double SP splits); extends `TritonCustomMaskFmha` to FP8 (`torch.float8_e4m3fn`) KV caches with tile-level `kv_scale_quant_orig` dequantization (**`0 B` BF16 pool copy, saving `8 MiB` on `128x16x2x512`**) and `[-448.0, 448.0]` output clamping; and threads Rust `return_token_ids`, clamps `VLLM_LOG_STATS_INTERVAL` to `Duration::from_nanos(1)` (preventing `tokio::time::interval(0)` panics), and centralizes `ModelArgs::file_sources()`. |
| `te-zero-wgrad-argmax-torchtitan-clip-hf-cursor-lmcache-nixl-mooncake` | [中文](docs/components/te-zero-wgrad-argmax-torchtitan-clip-hf-cursor-lmcache-nixl-mooncake.md) · [English](docs/components/te-zero-wgrad-argmax-torchtitan-clip-hf-cursor-lmcache-nixl-mooncake.en.md) | [`experiments/te-zero-wgrad-argmax-torchtitan-clip-hf-cursor-lmcache-nixl-mooncake/`](experiments/te-zero-wgrad-argmax-torchtitan-clip-hf-cursor-lmcache-nixl-mooncake/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`fc48751409`, `39a8b879df` & `e6afa9ddfa`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`9698a73362` & `c0eb136092`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`b28250e765` & `fcee51cc5e`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`4f42be7beb`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`3f6e1a2716`) & `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`605212cfca`, `5b74e87951`, `cc5875f2fc` & `ca8b4475bf`): replaces materialized dummy `wgrad` tensors with `torch._efficientzerotensor` (**`0 B` allocated storage, saving `3.76 GB` across 32 `4096x14336` BF16 layers**), adds NaN-safe tie-breaking `return_argmax=True` to `parallel_cross_entropy`, and preserves `GroupedLinear` `backward_objects`; moves `GradientClipper` into `OptimizersContainer` and buffers `_pending_hf_state` in `_HuggingFaceCursorIterator` (**preserving restored cursor `3` vs reverting to `0`**); adds `server_module_affinity_call` & Ruff `TID251` pickle ban in LMCache; sets a 2s SGLang HTTP pool timeout in Dynamo; ships NIXL `nixl::ucx::proxyEngine` (`128` shared workers for `32x4` rings & fenced `UCP_ATOMIC_OP_ADD`); and hardens Mooncake lease/birth-time/partial-bucket/DFS-shard invariants. |
| `flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-fp8pv-sm110-xqa-sm107-ssd-deepgemm-deslop` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-fp8pv-sm110-xqa-sm107-ssd-deepgemm-deslop.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-fp8pv-sm110-xqa-sm107-ssd-deepgemm-deslop.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-fp8pv-sm110-xqa-sm107-ssd-deepgemm-deslop/`](experiments/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-fp8pv-sm110-xqa-sm107-ssd-deepgemm-deslop/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`ddba2f4b2c`, `da1e9f4bc2`, `7601d0bb7e`, `597e3a67ca` & `4d79be4100`): adds SM120/SM121 FP8 (`e4m3`) PV support to MiniMax-H3 varlen attention via standalone V transpose/quantize kernels and a 4-step `m16n8k32.row.col.f32.e4m3.e4m3.f32` MMA2 loop (`228,440,928 B` at 64K / `749,614,176 B` at 210K workspace), achieving **`1.3814x` speedup on RTX 5090 (`19.63 -> 14.21 ms`) and `1.1876x` on RTX PRO 6000 (`61.20 -> 51.53 ms`)**; adds SM110 (`(11, 0)`) XQA `kernel="auto"` dispatching `'pair'` for non-speculative MHA & speculative GQA (`group_size=8`) and `'tmem'` otherwise; accelerates 6 Rubin (`sm_107a`) Mamba2 SSD programs (**`1.0839x` geomean main kernel, `1.268x` peak on R200**); and consolidates 50 Cake DeepGEMM FP8/batched programs onto `cake_host_common.cuh` (**`-12,408` net lines across `115` files**, `1.001x`/`1.000x` on GB300). |

| `flashinfer-cake-allgather-matmul-hopper-msa-vsa-sm90-tmem-lane-quarter-cta-pair-drain` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-allgather-matmul-hopper-msa-vsa-sm90-tmem-lane-quarter-cta-pair-drain.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-allgather-matmul-hopper-msa-vsa-sm90-tmem-lane-quarter-cta-pair-drain.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_allgather_hopper_msa_vsa_tmem_race/`](experiments/flashinfer_cake_allgather_hopper_msa_vsa_tmem_race/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`5fa42466bf`, `db8b7985fb`, `c17eb6e180`, `d7822fc387`, `4666987e4d`, `209061ab32`, `369222ab47`, `b6eb9591d4` & `02cf06743f`): routes SM100a `ws2` `1024x2048` (`K=8192`) `cake_all_gather_matmul` to `_cg2` (**`1.065x`, `101.9 -> 95.4 us`**) and allocates `16` SM-push CTAs/peer on SM100a `ws4` (`48` CTAs, **`1.086x`, `127.5 -> 113.7 us`**) and SM103a `ws8` (`112` CTAs, `1.034x`); lands `89` Hopper (`sm_90a`) MSA programs (**`3.237x` Top-K: `13.775 -> 4.256 us`**), Blackwell heavy-first GQA-folded union prefill + 8-warp Top-K (**`3.300x` GB300 / `2.727x` B200** on paged `first_topk`), and `17` Hopper VSA programs (**`1.2524x`**); fixes `tcgen05` `tmem_lane_base = (tid & 96) << 16` across 80 loads in `cake_dsa_train` (**restoring `128` vs `32` unique TMEM lanes**), warp-uniform `(consumer_warp * 32) << 16` across 66 `cake_alpha_moe` NVFP4 epilogue sites, and `producer_tail` draining `min(STAGES, K_steps)` `mma_done` barriers across `50` `cta_group::2` kernels. |
| `trtllm-mm-chunk-encoder-swaps-mma-spec-mask-kvcm2-progressive-vllm-ihc-sglang-xpu` | [中文](docs/components/trtllm-mm-chunk-encoder-swaps-mma-spec-mask-kvcm2-progressive-vllm-ihc-sglang-xpu.md) · [English](docs/components/trtllm-mm-chunk-encoder-swaps-mma-spec-mask-kvcm2-progressive-vllm-ihc-sglang-xpu.en.md) | [`experiments/trtllm_mm_chunk_spec_mask_kvcm2_vllm_ihc_sglang_xpu/`](experiments/trtllm_mm_chunk_spec_mask_kvcm2_vllm_ihc_sglang_xpu/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`3c64a85d4b`, `3d816f5355` & `f86fc83a61`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`fc18c48d88` & `3b6137f5ad`) & `sgl-project/sglang` (`a09c44ed45`, `0651d23b6f` & `448eb918e0`): schedules multimodal inputs as `ContextChunk` list with reference-counted `TensorLRUCache.releasable_bytes`, derives exact `getCustomMaskCapacityUpperBound` for `SwapsMmaAb` (**`4.0x` legacy `KeepsMmaAb` capacity: `2,097,152` vs `524,288` `uint32` words** at `B=64, Q=64, H=8`), and commits KVCM2 host memory progressively in `2..32 MiB` units (**`2,048x` smaller synchronous startup commit at `64 GiB`**); calls `omp_pause_resource_all(_OMP_PAUSE_HARD=2)` before `os.fork()` and fuses ROCm HY-V4 `triton_ihc_post_pre_rms_norm` (**`128 -> 80 KiB/token`, `1.60x` HBM reduction**); gates Intel XPU zero-copy Mooncake RDMA on `ptr & (1 << 63)` (`0` `EFAULT`), bounds CUTLASS MoE workspace by `min(T * dp, 8192)`, and threads `SGLANG_ZMQ_SOURCE_IP`. |
| `nixl-gpunetio-read-coalesce-ucx-cpu-proxy-mooncake-tent-abi-dynamo-planner-torchtitan-te-ep` | [中文](docs/components/nixl-gpunetio-read-coalesce-ucx-cpu-proxy-mooncake-tent-abi-dynamo-planner-torchtitan-te-ep.md) · [English](docs/components/nixl-gpunetio-read-coalesce-ucx-cpu-proxy-mooncake-tent-abi-dynamo-planner-torchtitan-te-ep.en.md) | [`experiments/nixl_gpunetio_read_ucx_proxy_mooncake_tent_dynamo_planner_torchtitan_te/`](experiments/nixl_gpunetio_read_ucx_proxy_mooncake_tent_dynamo_planner_torchtitan_te/README.md) | `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`5ef18e238e`, `e699ecbb3b` & `cb193c9c1c`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`b3de891c34` & `938f052ac9`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`2ba6e07f60`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`ca558e1201` & `6db08e59d2`) & `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`ebfd0e774a`): coalesces contiguous GPUNetIO READs across the 512-entry boundary up to `8 MiB` (**`4,096 -> 2` WQEs, `8 -> 2` ring entries, `+109.92%` throughput `10.97 -> 23.03 GB/s`, `-52.11%` p99 `1,516 -> 726 us` on 2×H20**) and ships UCX multi-threaded CPU Proxy EP (**`82.97 / 80.23 GB/s` dispatch/combine on 16×GB200** at 8ch/8thr); aligns Rust `TentRequest` to 48-byte C ABI (`priority@40`, `transport_hint@44`) and snapshots `TimeKey` before sorting; guards `/engine/planner` with HTTP 409 revision checks and `16 MiB`/`4,096`-step off-loop predictor warmup; activates `ChunkedLossWrapper` `head_has_trainable_params` for frozen-decoder `lm_head` LoRA and replaces TE JAX static EP handle cache with `ncclEpImportHandle`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-mhc-glm53-msa-kmajor-fp4-allreduce-multiwave-bmm-order` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-mhc-glm53-msa-kmajor-fp4-allreduce-multiwave-bmm-order.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-mhc-glm53-msa-kmajor-fp4-allreduce-multiwave-bmm-order.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_mhc_msa_kmajor_multiwave_bmm/`](experiments/flashinfer_cake_mhc_msa_kmajor_multiwave_bmm/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`130dd2429a`, `2ff6d8a5f2`, `3b65505f40`, `c7d1eacfdc`, `a3f2525e39` & `c1bcf70660`): implements 4-stream GLM-5.3-Flash mHC training (`streams=4, hidden=4096`, 19-step Sinkhorn-Knopp doubly stochastic mixing, `256 B/token` saved stats vs `0 B/token` checkpoint replay) on SM100/SM103/SM107, delivering **`15.95x` forward (`1454.7 us` vs `23197.5 us`) and `14.79x` checkpoint (`2823.8 us` vs `41773.9 us`) on GB300 at `T=65536`** (`14.28x` ckpt on R200); adds 3 persistent K-major prefill routes to `cake_blackwell_msa` (**`3.39x` GB300 geomean across `106` shapes**, peak `5.284x` paged prefill) and Round-2 Kimi-K3 AttnRes; overlaps TP2 row-scale reads and retains TP4 pass-1 validated NVFP4 packets in SMEM (**`3,472 -> 16 B/row` L2 re-read, `-99.54%`**); sizes 4-wave KV split `partialStats` by `max(SMs, totalNumCtas) * stepQ` (`9,216` vs `2,368`) and enforces strict weak ordering in BMM default selection (**`1.90x` on NVFP4 FC2: `0.135 -> 0.071 ms`**). |
| `trtllm-minimax-m3-bf16-split-router-flux-fp8-sglang-quark-asm-vllm-moriio` | [中文](docs/components/trtllm-minimax-m3-bf16-split-router-flux-fp8-sglang-quark-asm-vllm-moriio.md) · [English](docs/components/trtllm-minimax-m3-bf16-split-router-flux-fp8-sglang-quark-asm-vllm-moriio.en.md) | [`experiments/trtllm_minimax_m3_router_flux_sglang_quark_vllm_moriio/`](experiments/trtllm_minimax_m3_router_flux_sglang_quark_vllm_moriio/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`522daa8cdf`, `5d6fbb2062` & `5f90e8939d`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`6e98c84c60`, `ed268dd320` & `ca72dad953`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`db3b0f0fae` & `35d01660ff`): decomposes MiniMax-M3 FP32 router weights into `3` residual BF16 pieces `[E, 3H]` (`24` mantissa bits total), achieving **`0.0` (`< 1e-9`) logit drift vs `1.16e-4` TF32 drift** in a single BF16 tensor-core GEMM, plus SM103 `fusedDiTQKNormRopeQuantFp8Kernel` and custom-mask FP8 KV/out scaling; implements Quark AITER ASM FP4 7D E8M0 scale swizzling (`32x8` bijection, `256`-row `0x7F` padding, MI355X `N<=128` safety guard), DeepSeek-V4.1 vision-biased MoE routing simulation, and Rust response-ring health-probe acks; moves ROCm MoRIIO read completion await into `start_load_kv` before forward with `math.ceil(timeout * 1000)` ms deadline. |
| `dynamo-kv-state-agent-atomic-cursor-1000slot-nixl-multiqp-mooncake-ascend-mmap-torchtitan-mla-cp` | [中文](docs/components/dynamo-kv-state-agent-atomic-cursor-1000slot-nixl-multiqp-mooncake-ascend-mmap-torchtitan-mla-cp.md) · [English](docs/components/dynamo-kv-state-agent-atomic-cursor-1000slot-nixl-multiqp-mooncake-ascend-mmap-torchtitan-mla-cp.en.md) | [`experiments/dynamo_kv_cursor_1000slot_nixl_multiqp_torchtitan_mla_cp/`](experiments/dynamo_kv_cursor_1000slot_nixl_multiqp_torchtitan_mla_cp/README.md) | `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`93ff081d4e`, `9906dbe5ee` & `5235cfc93d`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`bb71164c4b`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`4137ac0026` & `7a05855674`) & `pytorch/torchtitan` (`0980e1cb1c` & `9698a73362`): decouples per-chunk `outbound_cursor: AtomicU64` from `ArcSwap<KvStateAgentStatus>` (**`10,002 -> 2` `ArcSwap::store` calls, `-99.98%`**) and pools `1,024` DP slots with 16-hex SHA-256 topics and `--slots-per-socket=8` (**`2,048 -> 256` ZMQ sockets, `8x` reduction**); stripes NIXL GPUNetIO RDMA WRITEs across `1..4` QPs in 1 launch with last-arrival tail posting and rebases Mooncake Ascend A5 `mmap`/`memfd` host segments; all-gathers compressed MLA latents `(kv_c_normed_TL, k_shared_TR)` (**`576` vs `40,960` elems/token, `71.11x` reduction**) and fuses Ulysses CP (`4 -> 2` All-to-Alls). |
| `flashinfer-masked-mxfp8-silu-mul-sm90-megamoe-push-cute-bmm-fp8-scale` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-masked-mxfp8-silu-mul-sm90-megamoe-push-cute-bmm-fp8-scale.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-masked-mxfp8-silu-mul-sm90-megamoe-push-cute-bmm-fp8-scale.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_masked_mxfp8_sm90_megamoe_bmm_fp8/`](experiments/flashinfer_masked_mxfp8_sm90_megamoe_bmm_fp8/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`140f9fc4c5`, `da992ed991`, `bf044ba86e` & `617845d1a3`): adds SM100+ `cvt_fp16_to_mxfp8_expert` & fused `silu_and_mul_mxfp8_experts_quantize` on B200 (`E=32, CAP=1024`), cutting GEMM1 quantize latency **`59.17 -> 5.90 us` (`10.03x` vs `cutile`)** and fused SiLU+Mul+MXFP8 **`91.22 -> 4.88 us` (`18.69x` at 16 valid rows/expert)**; fixes SM90 FP4 MegaMoE inactive-warp `fetch_expert_recv_count()` workspace cleanup (**`112 -> 0` dirty blocks** at 128 hot-expert tokens), enables H200 EP8 push dispatch (`M<=32` and `M=33..64` rotated pool, `~10 us/call`), and enforces `6 * scale <= 448` via power-of-two global scales; fuses `a_scale[0] * b_scale[0]` into `bmm_fp8` epilogue and gates `mm_mxfp8` warp split-K on `N // 16 <= SM_count` (`N <= 2368`), eliminating **`63/150` B300 and `43/120` B200 PDL regressions**. |
| `sglang-mooncake-failed-session-probe-glm52-side-stream-trtllm-glm53-sparse-sm107-mxfp8` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-mooncake-failed-session-probe-glm52-side-stream-trtllm-glm53-sparse-sm107-mxfp8.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-mooncake-failed-session-probe-glm52-side-stream-trtllm-glm53-sparse-sm107-mxfp8.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_mooncake_probe_glm52_trtllm_glm53_sparse/`](experiments/sglang_mooncake_probe_glm52_trtllm_glm53_sparse/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`be65391abd`, `2e7c9d851c`, `f7b8c6fe40` & `da7e3f1c12`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`ef913d39b3` & `4636b6311f`): default-enables Mooncake failed-session probe with exponential backoff (`30s -> 300s`) and `600s` idle pruning (**`2880 -> 4` probes/24h, `-99.86%`**) + per-GPU NIXL UCX `device_list` binding; records GLM-5.2 DSA side-stream work right before `wait_stream` for single-stream CUDA-graph replay and supports 1-to-many packed draft layers in `UMBPDirectLinker`; accelerates GLM-5.3-Flash `glm_kpool` via single-launch `_append_paged_state_kernel` (**`6 -> 1` launches**), 2D draft-verify same-pool skip (**`8 -> 3` updates**), and native TRTLLM-GEN FP8 decode up to `128` heads + Rubin `SM107` MXFP8 MoE. |
| `megatron-mfsdp-v2-execution-order-graph-grad-reuse-mooncake-shard-cursor-xpu-dmabuf` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-execution-order-graph-grad-reuse-mooncake-shard-cursor-xpu-dmabuf.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-execution-order-graph-grad-reuse-mooncake-shard-cursor-xpu-dmabuf.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_exec_order_grad_reuse_mooncake_cursor/`](experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_exec_order_grad_reuse_mooncake_cursor/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`60547a3f41`, `66a6d3f716` & `db3a6cd8a0`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`5eeb62a2fc`) & `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`0ed2fdfc68`, `e3e9d70eea` & `042ab8a18b`): replaces `IndexedOrder` with `ExecutionOrder` in MFSDP v2 to validate repeated module calls and look ahead without advancing the cursor, shares the forward/backward CUDA-graph pool in `FullIterationGradCopy` (**saving `4.0 GB` VRAM** on a 4 GB FP32 grad shard setup), and fixes EP MoE DTensor checkpointing; caches `self._op` in TE `LinearAttentionBase.__init__` (**`1 -> 0` Dynamo graph breaks**); replaces Mooncake `tenant_object_index_` with shard-backed `TenantCursor` without epoch-bumping `ref` re-locks and restores exact `CUcontext` in `CudaPlatform::copy()`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-gdp-nemotron4-nvfp4-4over6-slot-append-causal-conv1d-bwd` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-nemotron4-nvfp4-4over6-slot-append-causal-conv1d-bwd.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-nemotron4-nvfp4-4over6-slot-append-causal-conv1d-bwd.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_gdp_nvfp4_4over6_conv1d_bwd/`](experiments/flashinfer_cake_gdp_nvfp4_4over6_conv1d_bwd/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`fc1790f4ec`, `52fbbd090c`, `3821440929` & `7c866e85d3`): ships `cake_gated_delta_product_mtp` for Nemotron 4 (`K=128, V=64, n in {1..4}`) on `sm_100a`/`sm_103a` with `splitv8_tile8` (`T=1 & B*HV<=160`, 8 CTAs/head) and `lanerow` (`l8r4`/`l8r2`/`l4r4`, 1 CTA/head), winning **`48/48` shapes on GB200 (`1.01x–3.99x`, peak `301.4 -> 75.5 us`)** and **`47/48` on GB300 (`0.98x–3.95x`, peak `296.6 -> 75.1 us`)**; adds `nvfp4_4over6` block scale selection (`-99.65%` sub-peak clustered block MSE, `51` default kernels SASS-identical) and removes `.cpu()` scale syncs (**`499.4 -> 451.7 ms`, `-47.7 ms` / `-9.55%`** on SM80 20-layer Qwen3.6-35B-A3B NVFP4 eager prefill); updates `cake_causal_conv1d_train` backward channel routing (**`1.0164x` on GB300 `C=512`, `1.0331x` across 11 routed shapes on Rubin R200**). |
