| `megatron-hybrid-dsa-gqa-gdn-cudagraph-mooncake-store-reshard-tenant-quota-nixl` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-hybrid-dsa-gqa-gdn-cudagraph-mooncake-store-reshard-tenant-quota-nixl.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-hybrid-dsa-gqa-gdn-cudagraph-mooncake-store-reshard-tenant-quota-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_dsa_gqa_gdn_mooncake_store_reshard_quota/`](experiments/megatron_dsa_gqa_gdn_mooncake_store_reshard_quota/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`d14b699755` & `29e1a7b669`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`e2d6e8e0f2` & `44c4d9cf4a`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`03d9b74648`): adds hybrid `DSGroupedSelfAttention` + unscaled `raw_values` loss logging and routes GDN varlen prefill through `num_householder=1` `chunk_gated_delta_product_varlen` with fixed-capacity chunk descriptors for CUDA Graph replay; plans Mooncake Store KV reuse with `replica_ordinal==0` upload writer deduplication (**`-50.0%` writers** at 2x replication) + `HeadIntervalIndex.cover` target replica loads and two-word `Held/Carried` upsert quota accounting with slab watermark eviction; adds non-zero 64-bit `nixl.xfer_request_id` and `nixl.xfer_execution` telemetry counters. |
| `flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-mnnvl-collective-handle` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-mnnvl-collective-handle.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-mnnvl-collective-handle.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_mnnvl_handle/run.py`](experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_mnnvl_handle/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`adca7b87d2` & `a48bf8af3c`): `CakeMlaNvfp4PagedDecode` on NVIDIA Rubin (`sm_107a`) stores **`352 B/token`** (`256 B` E2M1 + `32 B` E4M3 scales + `64 B` E4M3 RoPE, **`-69.44%` vs BF16**), quantizes queries online (`356 B/row`), and schedules mixed 256-row 4-CTA units + 128-row 2-CTA pairs via `mla2_plan`, achieving **`1.044x` overall / `1.104x` `Q=4` geomean and `1.248x` peak (`1300.8 vs 1623.8 us`)** on pre-production R200 (`author-benchmark`); `choose_mnnvl_handle_type` & `allocate_mnnvl_handles` coordinate stateless collective handle selection, attempting `FABRIC` even when NVML `clusterUuid` is all-zero and collectively falling back to `POSIX_FD` with `cft_enabled=False` when IMEX channels are absent. |
| `sglang-pd-dp-role-switch-vllm-dsv41-replay-rows` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-pd-dp-role-switch-vllm-dsv41-replay-rows.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-pd-dp-role-switch-vllm-dsv41-replay-rows.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_vllm_dsv41_replay/run.py`](experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_vllm_dsv41_replay/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`af49ada16d`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`85752a43c3`): unblocks live PD role switching under `--dp-size > 1` and `--enable-dp-attention` using a two-phase `prepare_only=True` preflight before mutating scheduler role or tearing down KV transfer queues (**`0` split-brain / `0` torn-down queues** when Rank 3 fails precheck vs `3 Decode + 1 Prefill` under single-phase commit); includes `req.prompt_logprob_token_ids is not None` in `_prepare_deepseek_v41_replay_for_current_pass` so DeepSeek-V4.1 target-token scoring under `dcp_size=4` retains all **`16 / 16` prompt rows** instead of slicing to `1` row. |
| `megatron-lsmuon-ns-stream-te-align-mooncake-acc-quota-nixl` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-lsmuon-ns-stream-te-align-mooncake-acc-quota-nixl.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-lsmuon-ns-stream-te-align-mooncake-acc-quota-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_lsmuon_stream_mooncake_acc_quota_nixl/run.py`](experiments/megatron_lsmuon_stream_mooncake_acc_quota_nixl/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`6c956376e3` & `45b68f914c`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`3b08b77168` & `44c4d9cf4a`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`f8248873bd`): `LayerShardedMuon._iter_ns` yields `ns_batch_size=8` generator batches to cut 128-layer `[2048,2048]` peak transient buffer **`4096 -> 256 MiB` (`16.0x` reduction)** while TE `GroupedLinear` enforces `alignment=256`; Mooncake ACC dynamically bounds RDMA windows (`4..64 MiB`, `2 ms` drain target, RNR backoff, empty-flight oversize admit) and `TenantQuotaPolicy` tracks `Held + Carried` slots across overwrite (`owed=900 -> 500 B`); NIXL records `nixl::xfer.complete` immediately on synchronous `NIXL_SUCCESS` (**`50 / 50` trace parity**). |
| `flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-stepfun-pair-raster-moe-vllm-dsv41-replay` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-stepfun-pair-raster-moe-vllm-dsv41-replay.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-stepfun-pair-raster-moe-vllm-dsv41-replay.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_stepfun_moe_vllm_dsv41/`](experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_stepfun_moe_vllm_dsv41/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`adca7b87d2` & `fd4de5acce`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`85752a43c3`): adds Rubin (`sm_107a` / R200 208 SMs) Cake dense NVFP4 MLA paged decode over a **`352 B/token`** KV cache and **`356 B/row`** quantized query buffer with mixed 4-CTA (`50`) and 2-CTA (`4`) clusters (**`1.0436x` overall geomean, `1.1039x` Q4 geomean, `1.2483x` peak: `1623.8 -> 1300.8 us`** vs TokenSpeed MLA 2.0.3); regenerates `111` StepFun SwiGLU MoE kernels/arch on `sm_100a`/`sm_103a` with pair-rasterized BF16 FC1 (`2.0x` L2 weight reuse) and 2-CTA `nvfp4_bf16tok` FC2 (**`1.0323x` on B200, `1.0245x` on GB300** at `T=1024`); fixes DeepSeek-V4.1 `prompt_logprob_token_ids` replay to preserve all **`300` rows** vs `WINDOW=64` truncation. |
| `sglang-pd-dp-attention-role-switch-trtllm-dp-kv-event-flush-mooncake-zerocopy` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-pd-dp-attention-role-switch-trtllm-dp-kv-event-flush-mooncake-zerocopy.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-pd-dp-attention-role-switch-trtllm-dp-kv-event-flush-mooncake-zerocopy.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_trtllm_adp_kv_mooncake_zc/`](experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_trtllm_adp_kv_mooncake_zc/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`af49ada16d`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`ec782566f5` & `69e06b8afb`): enables PD role switching under `dp_size > 1` and `--enable-dp-attention` via a two-phase `prepare_only=True` preflight (**`0` torn-down ranks** on partial OOM vs `3/4` split-brain); moves `_add_kv_cache_events()` outside rank-divergent `_process_previous_batch()` and runs rebalance collectives on `absent` ADP ranks (**`3/3` transcripts deadlock-free**); enables Mooncake Store zero-copy (`stage_through_host=False`) by cutting V2 VMM pools and straddling pages at `cuMemCreate` boundaries (`[2 MiB, 2 MiB]`), saving **`1,024 MiB` pinned host DRAM** per 8-GPU node. |
| `megatron-layersharded-muon-stream-te-cublasmp-nvfp4-mooncake-rdma-cc-nixl-xfer` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-layersharded-muon-stream-te-cublasmp-nvfp4-mooncake-rdma-cc-nixl-xfer.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-layersharded-muon-stream-te-cublasmp-nvfp4-mooncake-rdma-cc-nixl-xfer.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_muon_stream_te_cublasmp_nvfp4_mooncake_cc_nixl/`](experiments/megatron_muon_stream_te_cublasmp_nvfp4_mooncake_cc_nixl/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`6c956376e3`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`6b5cd7e2e4`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`3b08b77168`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`f8248873bd`): `LayerShardedMuon._iter_ns` streams local batched Newton-Schulz with in-place scaling on single-rank MoE domains, cutting NS launches **`17 -> 5` (`3.4x`)** and peak transient FP32 memory **`1,088.0 -> 192.0 MiB` (`5.6667x`)**; TE `cuBLASMp >= 0.11.0` adds NVFP4 (`VEC16_UE4M3`) overlap via **`12 B`** device pointer-mode scalars `{alpha, 0, 1}`, `(N/tp)%128!=0` fallback, and single dual-usage `grad_output` quantization; Mooncake adds `[4, 64] MiB` AIMD RDMA congestion control and NIXL emits **`50/50`** `nixl::xfer.complete` UCX marks. |
| `flashinfer-cake-grouped-mxfp8-quantize-vllm-dsv4-eplb-redundant-slots` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-grouped-mxfp8-quantize-vllm-dsv4-eplb-redundant-slots.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-grouped-mxfp8-quantize-vllm-dsv4-eplb-redundant-slots.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_grouped_mxfp8_vllm_dsv4_eplb_kimi/`](experiments/flashinfer_cake_grouped_mxfp8_vllm_dsv4_eplb_kimi/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`d8ddf1a367`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`9d4192aaa1` & `56e67b46a3`): routes `cake_grouped_mxfp8_quantize` through the in-tree `CakeProgram` loader (`d8aa920df5a6252450bd` BF16, `f84d4eab2905894ac4c2` FP16 via `a.view(torch.bfloat16)`) on `sm_100a` and `sm_103a` with in-kernel 128-column padding, `[B, pm_tiles, pk_tiles, 32, 4, 4]` E8M0 scale layout, and **`3.808/4.000 us` (B200) and `3.776/3.648 us` (GB300)** latency (`0.983x–1.000x`); forwards `num_redundant_experts` in DeepSeek-V4 `get_expert_mapping()` and verifies byte-sum parity via `check_eplb_replica_slots` (**`8 -> 0` unloaded redundant slots**); configures Kimi-K3 across four 4-GPU GB200 trays as **`TP8 x PP2 + DCP8 (a2a)`** (`-50%` PP stages) with `TRTLLM_RAGGED` MLA prefill and `K=4` DSpark. |
| `sglang-detok-batch-outputs-radix-path-child-count-dynamo-coredump` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-detok-batch-outputs-radix-path-child-count-dynamo-coredump.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-detok-batch-outputs-radix-path-child-count-dynamo-coredump.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_detok_batch_radix_child_count_dynamo_coredump/`](experiments/sglang_detok_batch_radix_child_count_dynamo_coredump/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`468e26f253` & `dc62c3effd`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`b169bcc08a`): `SGLANG_BATCH_TOKENIZER_OUTPUTS=True` groups non-contiguous batch outputs by tokenizer worker IPC in first-seen owner order (**`12 -> 3` ZMQ IPC sends, `4.0x` reduction** across 12 requests and 3 workers) with `False` opt-out; extends `TreePathNodeInfo` in Python and Rust `UnifiedTreeCore.collect_path_info` to a 6-tuple exposing `num_children` (`1 -> 2` after branching); `_default_cuda_coredump_wait` sets `SGLANG_CUDA_COREDUMP_BEFORE_CRASH_WAIT_SECS="0"` when coredumps are disabled, saving **60s** on `SIGQUIT`. |
| `megatron-mfsdp-v2-sharded-muon-offline-kd-nixl-prometheus-mp` | [中文](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-sharded-muon-offline-kd-nixl-prometheus-mp.md) · [English](docs/components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-sharded-muon-offline-kd-nixl-prometheus-mp.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_muon_offline_kd_nixl_prometheus/`](experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_muon_offline_kd_nixl_prometheus/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`91d774de79` & `fce1e2051f`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`60dd8b7cd0`): implements MFSDP v2 `FsdpMuon` with sharded `DTensor` `RowAtomic` momentum, 4 weight-decay modes + Nesterov `lerp`, and owner-compute orthogonalization (**`0.0` numerical drift** vs unsharded Muon); upgrades offline logits KD to v2 with sample-range tars, `v2_pack_indices` 1-bit/elem 17th-bit packing (**`-87.5%` bit-17 storage, `-29.17%` total**), per-document CP zigzag slicing/reassembly, and `tp0/cp0/dp0` tracker ownership; aliases NIXL `prometheus` to `prometheus_mp` behind one `/dev/shm` multi-rank endpoint. |
| `flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-epilogue-nvfp4-svdquant-moe-stream` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-epilogue-nvfp4-svdquant-moe-stream.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-epilogue-nvfp4-svdquant-moe-stream.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_minimax_h3_sm120_epilogue_nvfp4_svdquant_moe/`](experiments/flashinfer_minimax_h3_sm120_epilogue_nvfp4_svdquant_moe/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`5805975da0`, `883186fa77` & `84db4f08d6`): replacing scalar element-by-element global stores in SM120/SM121 MiniMax-H3 fused MoE finalize (`H=4096`) with 128-bit `uint4` (`8 x bf16`) vectorized stores cuts store transactions **`-87.5%` (`4,096 -> 512` per token-expert)** and achieves **`1.0702x` geomean (`1.1992x` peak: `147 -> 122 us` at `M=16`, `1.1869x` at `M=64`)** on RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell; fusing NVFP4 with INT4 `r=32` SVDQuant low-rank residual inside CUTLASS fused MoE cuts quantization MSE **`-59.09%`** (`0.000516 -> 0.000211`) for `+15.625%` weight bytes (`12,058,624 B` vs `10,485,760 B` workspace via `max(128, r)`); `cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&s, cudaStreamNonBlocking)` eliminates default-stream-`0` synchronization stalls. |
| `sglang-dspark-sim-default-radix-split-storage-hit-zmq-allowlist` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-dspark-sim-default-radix-split-storage-hit-zmq-allowlist.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-dspark-sim-default-radix-split-storage-hit-zmq-allowlist.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_dspark_default_radix_split_storage_load_zmq/`](experiments/sglang_dspark_default_radix_split_storage_load_zmq/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`e088d9c8e9` & `7b5a044a75`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`cf14483f82`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`24f275c38b`): enabling DSpark simulated acceptance by default skips **`36,495,360` vocab softmax elements/step** (`B=32, K=6, V=152,064`), cutting step verification **`13.489 -> 0.170 ms` (`79.13x`)**; `_split_node` preserves `storage_backed=True` on split internal parents (`evictable_size=1,024`, `reclaimable_len=0`), recovering **`2,048` vs `0` L3 hit tokens** after child eviction; `LoadOrchestrator` deduplicates 16 concurrent 64-block loads to cut storage block reads **`-87.5%` (`640 -> 80` vs `80` unique)**; Dynamo ZMQ rejects `Pickle`/`Cloudpickle` by default, requiring explicit `DYNAMO_ALLOW_UNSAFE_SERIALIZATION=1`. |
| `torchtitan-dist-moe-swiglu-rl-cp-te-cublasmp-wgrad-megatron-mfsdp` | [中文](docs/components/torchtitan-dist-moe-swiglu-rl-cp-te-cublasmp-wgrad-megatron-mfsdp.md) · [English](docs/components/torchtitan-dist-moe-swiglu-rl-cp-te-cublasmp-wgrad-megatron-mfsdp.en.md) | [`experiments/torchtitan_dist_moe_swiglu_rl_cp_te_cublasmp_mfsdp/`](experiments/torchtitan_dist_moe_swiglu_rl_cp_te_cublasmp_mfsdp/README.md) | `pytorch/torchtitan` (`1fc7d3184a` & `02165d6de2`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`31b761dc05`) & `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`6fd6a0a5c8`): restoring `w1`/`w3` layout before `_run_moe_Comm` under `use_custom_ops` eliminates `gate`/`up` chunk permutation (**`0.0` vs `27.95` L1 error**); Unified RL CP gathers per-shard `logprobs` via `funcol.all_gather_tensor` and aligns `labels[:, 1:]` before loss masking (**`0.0` vs `0.659656` loss bias** at `CP=4`); TE `cuBLASMp` caches the `dgrad` AllGather buffer for `wgrad`, halving backward AllGather traffic (**`128 -> 64 MiB/layer`, `-50.0%`**); Megatron bypasses `check_Unexpected` under `use_megatron_fsdp` to avoid false-positive `_fsdp_wrapped_module` failures. |
| `flashinfer-cake-ssd-vcount-mla-tmem-lane-fmha-planfree-dcp` | [中文](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-ssd-vcount-mla-tmem-lane-fmha-planfree-dcp.md) · [English](docs/components/flashinfer-cake-ssd-vcount-mla-tmem-lane-fmha-planfree-dcp.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_ssd_vcount_mla_tmem_fmha_planfree/`](experiments/flashinfer_cake_ssd_vcount_mla_tmem_fmha_planfree/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`4af20723d2`, `bc45bb9878` & `fda1694904`): `_chunk_parallel_vcount(capability, sm_count)` caps Mamba2 SSD look-back virtual CTAs at `min(table_vcount, sm_count)` (`212 -> 208` on 208-SM Rubin R200 `sm_107a`), eliminating the 4-CTA tail wave for **`1.345x–1.641x` warm (`301.68 -> 183.87 us` on `1x65536` bf16) and `1.512x–1.639x` cold speedups**; 9 Blackwell `cta_group::2` Cake MLA decode kernels replace `(n_half * 64)` with `(((warp % 4) * 32) << 16)` so warps 2 & 3 read keys `64..127` from their own TMEM lane quarter, cutting `_INPUT_SCALE=0.5` max abs error **`3.2e-2` (`12.2%` mismatch) -> `3.1e-4`** at `11.84 us`; `dcp_balanced_program` routes E4M3 `head_dim=128` DCP speculative decode (`prod_b1_s8192_q4_cp4_graph`, `136 <= 148` tickets) to plan-free static one-wave `Program 2` (**`12.58 -> 10.85 us` on B200, `1.159x`**). |
| `sglang-dspark-sim-accept-radix-reclaimable-subpool-vllm-rocm-gated-norm` | [中文](docs/components/sglang-dspark-sim-accept-radix-reclaimable-subpool-vllm-rocm-gated-norm.md) · [English](docs/components/sglang-dspark-sim-accept-radix-reclaimable-subpool-vllm-rocm-gated-norm.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_dspark_sim_accept_radix_reclaimable_subpool_vllm_rocm/`](experiments/sglang_dspark_sim_accept_radix_reclaimable_subpool_vllm_rocm/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`7d9bc10493`, `0b536ca112` & `bab4a808d8`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`9da5f2d310`): dropping `_is_hip` on DSpark `accept_sampling_info = None`, `simulate_fold`, and `_simulate_acc_greedy` skips **`24,330,240` vocab softmax elements/step** on CUDA (`B=16, K=5, V=152,064`); `UnifiedTreeCore` and `sglang-radix-tree` separate logical `value_len` from allocator-backed `reclaimable_value_len` (**`0` ledger drift vs `-1,024` underflow**) with `<0` `ValueError` guards; `MultiEndedAllocator` lazily sorts `_free_phys_pages` and drains from `"low"` (`grow="up"`) or `"high"` (`grow="down"`), cutting opposite-end pollution **`2 -> 0`** and recovering **`4` vs `0` contiguous gap pages**; vLLM ROCm registers `torch.ops.vllm.fla_rms_norm_gated` and enables `+rms_norm_gated` by default when `quantization is None`. |
| `torchtitan-dsv3-hf-save-map-gpt-oss-mxfp4-dynamo-sglang-elastic-ep-nixl` | [中文](docs/components/torchtitan-dsv3-hf-save-map-gpt-oss-mxfp4-dynamo-sglang-elastic-ep-nixl.md) · [English](docs/components/torchtitan-dsv3-hf-save-map-gpt-oss-mxfp4-dynamo-sglang-elastic-ep-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/torchtitan_dsv3_hf_save_map_gpt_oss_mxfp4_dynamo_elastic_ep/`](experiments/torchtitan_dsv3_hf_save_map_gpt_oss_mxfp4_dynamo_elastic_ep/README.md) | `pytorch/torchtitan` (`1d581795cd` & `da54a64ede`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`02da7f4486` & `c8b3a8e0e5`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`cc6b43e989`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`56c1c2f706`): `DeepSeekV3StateDictAdapter` filters the `91,991`-entry index to **`45,395` (`M=0`) or `46,183` (`M=1`)** (dropping `45,808` `.weight_scale_inv` keys) and clones `_MTP_SHARED_COPIES` (`3.71 GB`); `GptOssStateDictAdapter` separates input-first BF16 `gate_up_proj [E, D, 2F]` from output-first MXFP4 `_blocks`; Dynamo aligns 5 SGLang Elastic-EP NIXL invariants (`deep_gemm`, `BF16_DISPATCH=1`, `MAX_TOKENS=1024`, `dense-tp=1`) and switches TRT-LLM snapshots to OpenMPI `ob1`; Mooncake adds per-key `route::Generation` accessors and NIXL bumps Trace Plugin API to V2. |

## 4. Verification & Reproducibility Commands

All validation tools and experiments use the Python 3.12 standard library only:

```powershell
# 1. Run the full unit test suite (485 unit tests)
python -m unittest discover -s tests

# 2. Validate research queue, evidence ledger, source citations, and links
python scripts\validate_research.py
```
